# Diplomacy only: no production cheats, forced faction membership, declarations or peace effects.
# All profiles are reversible and delegate the final legality/acceptance to HOI4 diplomacy.
# Partner bindings include the native civil-war pool D01-D50.
terra_dip_avoid_costly_new_wars = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_ready = yes OR = { terra_dip_exhausted = yes has_war = yes } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = avoid_starting_wars value = 100 }
}

terra_dip_goodwill_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_AGI > 29 } check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_AGI < 50 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = befriend id = AGI value = 45 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = AGI target = improve_relation value = 25 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = AGI target = military_access value = 20 }
}

terra_dip_cooperation_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } is_subject = no AGI = { is_subject = no } check_variable = { terra_dip_trust_AGI > 39 } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_AGI > 44 } check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_AGI < 40 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = alliance id = AGI value = 35 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = AGI target = offer_join_faction value = 25 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = AGI target = join_faction value = 20 }
}

terra_dip_restraint_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_can_offer_restraint = { TARGET = AGI } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = AGI target = non_aggression_pact value = 10 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = AGI target = non_aggression_pact value = 10 }
}

terra_dip_hostility_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = AGI } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_AGI < -34 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = antagonize id = AGI value = 40 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = AGI target = offer_join_faction value = -60 }
}

terra_dip_contain_threat_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } terra_dip_military_policy_allowed = yes is_neighbor_of = AGI check_variable = { terra_dip_score_AGI < -24 } NOT = { strength_ratio = { tag = AGI ratio > 0.9 } } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = AGI } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = contain id = AGI value = 65 }
    ai_strategy = { type = declare_war id = AGI value = -100 }
}

terra_dip_prepare_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } terra_dip_can_prepare_against = { TARGET = AGI } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = AGI value = 45 }
}

terra_dip_seek_goal_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } terra_dip_can_seek_war_against = { TARGET = AGI } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = conquer id = AGI value = 35 }
}

terra_dip_legal_war_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } terra_dip_can_seek_war_against = { TARGET = AGI } has_wargoal_against = AGI can_declare_war_on = AGI }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = declare_war id = AGI value = 80 }
}

terra_dip_subject_duty_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA original_tag = TNJ } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } is_subject_of = AGI AGI = { has_war = yes } NOT = { has_war_together_with = AGI } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = AGI target = call_allies value = 100 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = AGI target = join_allies value = 100 }
}

terra_dip_defend_partner_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA original_tag = TNJ } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } OR = { is_in_faction_with = AGI is_subject_of = AGI AGI = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } AGI = { has_defensive_war = yes } NOT = { has_war_together_with = AGI } check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_AGI < 70 } NOT = { terra_dip_exhausted = yes } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = AGI target = call_allies value = 80 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = AGI target = join_allies value = 60 }
}

terra_dip_offensive_partner_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA original_tag = TNJ } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } OR = { is_in_faction_with = AGI is_subject_of = AGI AGI = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } NOT = { has_war_together_with = AGI } terra_dip_can_support_offensive_war = { TARGET = AGI } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = AGI target = call_allies value = 25 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = AGI target = join_allies value = 20 }
}

terra_dip_decline_costly_call_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } OR = { is_in_faction_with = AGI is_subject_of = AGI AGI = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } NOT = { is_subject_of = AGI } AGI = { has_war = yes } NOT = { has_war_together_with = AGI } OR = { terra_dip_exhausted = yes check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_AGI > 69 } AND = { AGI = { has_offensive_war = yes } NOT = { terra_dip_can_support_offensive_war = { TARGET = AGI } } } } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = AGI target = call_allies value = -80 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = AGI target = join_allies value = -60 }

terra_dip_call_partner_AGI = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } NOT = { has_war_with = AGI } OR = { is_in_faction_with = AGI is_subject_of = AGI AGI = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } has_war = yes NOT = { has_war_together_with = AGI } AGI = { NOT = { terra_dip_exhausted = yes } } OR = { has_defensive_war = yes check_variable = { terra_dip_trust_AGI > 39 } AGI = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } }

    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = conquer id = SRC value = 35 }
}

    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = SRC target = join_allies value = 100 }
}

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_can_offer_restraint = { TARGET = YAN } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = YAN target = non_aggression_pact value = 10 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = YAN target = non_aggression_pact value = 10 }
}

terra_dip_hostility_YAN = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = YAN } NOT = { has_war_with = YAN } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = YAN } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_YAN < -34 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = antagonize id = YAN value = 40 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = YAN target = offer_join_faction value = -60 }
}

terra_dip_contain_threat_YAN = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = YAN } NOT = { has_war_with = YAN } terra_dip_military_policy_allowed = yes is_neighbor_of = YAN check_variable = { terra_dip_score_YAN < -24 } NOT = { strength_ratio = { tag = YAN ratio > 0.9 } } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = YAN } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = contain id = YAN value = 65 }
    ai_strategy = { type = declare_war id = YAN value = -100 }
}

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = LGL } NOT = { has_war_with = LGL } terra_dip_military_policy_allowed = yes has_war = no NOT = { terra_dip_exhausted = yes } LGL = { is_subject = no } is_neighbor_of = LGL check_variable = { terra_dip_trust_LGL > 49 } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_LGL > 49 } NOT = { has_guaranteed = LGL } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = protect id = LGL value = 40 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = LGL target = guarantee value = 25 }
}

    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D10 target = call_allies value = 100 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D10 target = join_allies value = 100 }
}

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D16 } NOT = { has_war_with = D16 } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_D16 > 29 } check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_D16 < 50 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = befriend id = D16 value = 45 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D16 target = improve_relation value = 25 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D16 target = military_access value = 20 }
}

terra_dip_cooperation_D16 = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D16 } NOT = { has_war_with = D16 } is_subject = no D16 = { is_subject = no } check_variable = { terra_dip_trust_D16 > 39 } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_D16 > 44 } check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_D16 < 40 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = alliance id = D16 value = 35 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D16 target = offer_join_faction value = 25 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D16 target = join_faction value = 20 }
}

terra_dip_restraint_D16 = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_can_offer_restraint = { TARGET = D16 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D16 target = non_aggression_pact value = 10 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D16 target = non_aggression_pact value = 10 }
}

terra_dip_hostility_D16 = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D16 } NOT = { has_war_with = D16 } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = D16 } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_D16 < -34 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = antagonize id = D16 value = 40 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D16 target = offer_join_faction value = -60 }
}

terra_dip_contain_threat_D16 = {
    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }
    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D16 } NOT = { has_war_with = D16 } terra_dip_military_policy_allowed = yes is_neighbor_of = D16 check_variable = { terra_dip_score_D16 < -24 } NOT = { strength_ratio = { tag = D16 ratio > 0.9 } } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = D16 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = contain id = D16 value = 65 }
    ai_strategy = { type = declare_war id = D16 value = -100 }
}

    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D18 } NOT = { has_war_with = D18 } OR = { is_in_faction_with = D18 is_subject_of = D18 D18 = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } NOT = { is_subject_of = D18 } D18 = { has_war = yes } NOT = { has_war_together_with = D18 } OR = { terra_dip_exhausted = yes check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_D18 > 69 } AND = { D18 = { has_offensive_war = yes } NOT = { terra_dip_can_support_offensive_war = { TARGET = D18 } } } } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D18 target = call_allies value = -80 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D18 target = join_allies value = -60 }
}

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D27 } NOT = { has_war_with = D27 } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_D27 > 29 } check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_D27 < 50 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = befriend id = D27 value = 45 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D27 target = improve_relation value = 25 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D27 target = military_access value = 20 }
}

    allowed = { NOT = { OR = { original_tag = SST original_tag = SEA } } }

    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D27 target = join_faction value = 20 }
}

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D29 } NOT = { has_war_with = D29 } OR = { is_in_faction_with = D29 is_subject_of = D29 D29 = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } NOT = { is_subject_of = D29 } D29 = { has_war = yes } NOT = { has_war_together_with = D29 } OR = { terra_dip_exhausted = yes check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_D29 > 69 } AND = { D29 = { has_offensive_war = yes } NOT = { terra_dip_can_support_offensive_war = { TARGET = D29 } } } } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D29 target = call_allies value = -80 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D29 target = join_allies value = -60 }
}

    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D32 target = call_allies value = 100 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D32 target = join_allies value = 100 }
}

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D38 } NOT = { has_war_with = D38 } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_D38 > 29 } check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_D38 < 50 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = befriend id = D38 value = 45 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D38 target = improve_relation value = 25 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D38 target = military_access value = 20 }
}

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D40 } NOT = { has_war_with = D40 } OR = { is_in_faction_with = D40 is_subject_of = D40 D40 = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } NOT = { is_subject_of = D40 } D40 = { has_war = yes } NOT = { has_war_together_with = D40 } OR = { terra_dip_exhausted = yes check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_D40 > 69 } AND = { D40 = { has_offensive_war = yes } NOT = { terra_dip_can_support_offensive_war = { TARGET = D40 } } } } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D40 target = call_allies value = -80 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D40 target = join_allies value = -60 }
}

    enable = { is_ai = yes terra_dip_pair_valid = { TARGET = D49 } NOT = { has_war_with = D49 } check_variable = { terra_dip_score_D49 > 29 } check_variable = { terra_dip_grievance_D49 < 50 } }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = { type = befriend id = D49 value = 45 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D49 target = improve_relation value = 25 }
    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_acceptance id = D49 target = military_access value = 20 }
}

    ai_strategy = { type = diplo_action_desire id = D49 target = join_faction value = 20 }
}
